# Kennel Onboarding — Run Log

Append one entry per daily run (newest at top). Keep it terse.

Format:
```
## YYYY-MM-DD — <shortName> (<region>)
- Source: <TYPE> — <url/id>
- Outcome: <PR #123 | branch onboard/... ready to push | blocked: reason>
- Events verified: <count>, date range <…>
- Historical backfill: <count or none>
- Follow-ups: <anything deferred>
```

> **✅ POST-MERGE LIVE (2026-07-16, all prod-verified) — HC BATCH-6.** [PR #2654](https://github.com/johnrclem/hashtracks-web/pull/2654)
> merged (`71d5ca1f`); all 6 kennels live: **rio-h3 3 · poznan-h3 3 · divahhh 9 · steel-city-h3 1 ·
> manchester-h3 3 · douliu-h3 31** canonical events (46 backfill + 4 live), **0 cancelled on every
> source** (the `upcomingOnly` reconcile contract held), correct region badges, all 6 `/kennels/*` +
> all 6 `/kennel-logos/*` serve 200. Scoped **Pass 3** created the 4 `scheduleRules` (mh3-gb 3 ·
> douliu-h3 1) via `runKennelSeedPass` + `applyUpserts` (Taoyuan precedent — NOT the global
> `runScheduleRuleBackfill`). Steel City & Manchester shipped **live-only** (0 backfill); the other 4
> are backfill + 0-upcoming (recently-active, expected). Divahhh landed **9** — the same-day #39/#40
> Walkers/Runners collapse guard held in the real merge (both survived, not 8).
>
> 🔴 **Incident during the post-merge seed — importing `prisma/seed.ts` auto-runs the FULL seed.** The
> scoped-seed one-shot `import`ed `seedKennels` from `prisma/seed.ts`, whose **unguarded top-level
> `main().catch()`** fired the entire full seed as an import side effect, running **concurrently** with
> the scoped call against prod. That concurrency (two racing `region.create`s) produced misleading
> `P2002` "unique on (name)" errors on names that didn't exist — not a driver bug, self-inflicted. The
> one-shot's `process.exit(0)` then killed the runaway `main()` **very early, right after region
> creation**, so the blast radius was near-zero: only the 6 batch kennels were touched (no other kennel
> profile overwritten), **zero** other source configs changed (the 17 other sources touched in the hour
> were all routine scrape-cron), and `deactivateStaleRules` never ran (schedule rules 447→451 = only the
> +4 mine, 78 inactive unchanged). **Fix follow-up: [PR #2655](https://github.com/johnrclem/hashtracks-web/pull/2655) adds an entry-point guard to
> `prisma/seed.ts`** so importing it is inert (mirrors `scripts/backfill-schedule-rules.ts`). Lesson:
> never `import` from `prisma/seed.ts` in a one-shot; write self-contained upserts, or import from the
> entry-point-guarded `scripts/backfill-schedule-rules.ts` for scoped Pass 3.

> new adapter code; **5 new METROs** (Rio de Janeiro / Poznan / Sheffield / Manchester / Douliu; Brussels

> Poznan / Divahhh / Douliu** (expected — recently-active). 0 errors, 0 unmatched. tsc + lint + **9,996
> tests** green. Retro `handoffs/retros/2026-07-15-hc-batch-6-retro.md`.
>
> 🔴 **Root cause of the backlog — the daily runs' output was never committed.** All six handoffs (plus
> their run-log entries, 4 platform notes and queue rows 12–15) sat **untracked/uncommitted in the main
> repo**; `origin/main` never moved, so any session branching from `origin/main` saw a "complete"
> handoffs folder and an audit wrongly concluded *"0 kennels left to onboard"* while six live kennels sat
> unimplemented on disk. **This is the same failure the 2026-07-05 catch-up diagnosed** — and whose fix
> ("teach `copy-newest-handoff.sh` to warn when >1 un-implemented handoff exists / add `--oldest`") was
> never built. It recurred at 6× scale. **Fix the pipeline: the daily run must COMMIT its handoff + doc
> edits**, and make the backlog loud.
>
> **Corrections applied during implementation:** (1) 🔴 both GB handoffs said `country: "United Kingdom"`
> — every existing UK kennel + the Cornwall METRO use **`"UK"`** (only `STATE_GROUP_MAP` *values* use
> "United Kingdom") → shipped `"UK"`. (2) 🔴 **Rio shipped 3 region.ts edits, not 4** — the handoff's
> `COUNTRY_GROUP_MAP` metro key is a **dead no-op** (`getCountryGroup` only ever receives
> `getStateGroup`'s output, which already maps Rio→Brazil); the São Paulo row omits it too. *The identical
> mistake hc-batch-4's retro already documented — the research prompt still emits it.* (3) 🔴 **Divahhh
> #39/#40** (Walkers/Runners, same date **and** same 09:30) would have been **silently collapsed** by
> merge (`looksLikeSameEvent` = equal startTime OR runNumber) → cleared the Walkers row's `startTime` so
> both survive; a collapse scanner now reports 0 unresolved pairs across all 4 backfills. (4) Douliu title
> cleaning needed a fragment guard ("DH3 #160 in Douliu" → "in Douliu" is worse than the synth) → 14 real
> themes kept, 17 synthesize. (5) Bandung anchor cited `:7443`/`:7463`, actually **`:7487`** — locate by
> content. (6) 🔴 **Steel City shipped FLAT schedule fields, dropping the handoff's `BYDAY=4SA`** — the
> observed runs fit neither ordinal (2025-05-24 = 4th-not-last; 2025-08-30 = last-not-4th), so a
> HIGH-confidence rule would mis-project every 5-Saturday month and the weekday-based rule-drift cron
> couldn't catch it (Algarve/Lune Valley flat precedent).
>
> **Post-merge = targeted seed + 🔴 SCOPED Pass-3 + 4 backfill runs + 6 scrapes.** 🔴 A targeted
> `seedKennels` does **NOT** create ScheduleRule rows — `runScheduleRuleBackfill` runs only from a full
> `prisma db seed` (`prisma/seed.ts:755`), which we avoid (barbados #2471 config-revert). So mh3-gb (×3)
> and douliu-h3 (×1) need a **scoped** Pass 3 — `runKennelSeedPass(prisma, planned, {}, <2-kennel filter>)`
> + `applyUpserts`, never the global (it touches all 462 rules + `deactivateStaleRules`) — the Taoyuan
> Metro precedent. Without it their rules land nowhere and nothing errors. SHIPPED flips below.

> `adapter.fetch` (titsh3 3 upcoming · lh3-fr 1 · heraultics 1 · krashh3 0 recently-active; 0 errors,
> 0 unmatched). **Corrections applied during implementation:** (1) **no `COUNTRY_GROUP_MAP` edits** —
> the handoffs said to add metro keys (`"Lyon":"France"`) there, but the group keys France/United
> Kingdom already exist; a metro key is a dead no-op (grouping resolves metro→group via
> `STATE_GROUP_MAP`, then group→country via `COUNTRY_GROUP_MAP`). (2) France inference added
> `montpellier|herault` but **omitted bare `lyon`** (it doubles as US Lyon County KS/NV/IA/MN/KY —
> `france` still catches "Lyon, France"), per the Warsaw/Belgrade discipline and the handoff's own
> caveat. tsc + lint + 9959 tests green. Retro `handoffs/retros/2026-07-09-hc-batch-4-retro.md`.
> SHIPPED flips below; post-merge = targeted seed + 4 backfill runs + scrape.

> **`lima-h3`** (Peru) is the one that stays blocked — re-verified dormant 2026-07-09: the primary blog `limahashash.blogspot.com`
> newest post is **Hash 765 / 2023-09-07 (~1036 days stale, 8 posts total)**, and the sibling
> `limahash.com` (Lima Extra Miércoles, WordPress) last real post is **2024-02-10 (~881 days)**; the
> page's only "2026" string is today's date. No live Lima source → not onboarded (honors the handoff's
> own "don't ship a dormant source" directive). **`tymh3-tw`** (Taoyuan, Taiwan) was blocked across two
> headless attempts — then **SHIPPED 2026-07-09** ([PR #2636](https://github.com/johnrclem/hashtracks-web/pull/2636),
> new `BoomCalendarAdapter`). 🔑 **The block was a platform MISID:** the "Calendar" widget is **not** Wix
> Events (that collection is empty — `wix_events_list` → `{"events":[]}`) — it's a third-party **Boom
> Calendar** Wix app (`appDefId 13b4a028-…`, iframe `calendar.boomte.ch`) with its own backend at
> `calendar.apiboomtech.com`. Because the fetch is inside a cross-origin iframe it's invisible to SSR /
> the parent network log, so browserRender (timed out / 429'd) and the Wix Events API (empty collection)
> were both dead ends. Handing the live page to **Claude-in-Chrome** identified Boom Calendar and the
> clean data path: `GET <site>/_api/v1/access-tokens` → `apps["13b4a028-…"].instance`, then
> `GET calendar.apiboomtech.com/api/calendar?comp_id=<compId>&instance=<jwt>` returns all events with
> venue coords in one call. Shipped as a reusable config-driven `BoomCalendarAdapter` (mints a fresh
> instance per scrape) — Taiwan's 6th metro (Taoyuan). Live-verified 3 upcoming (#181–#183, biweekly Fri
> 19:15). Retro `handoffs/retros/2026-07-09-tymh3-tw-retro.md`. **Lesson: live-verify against a real
> browser before concluding a JS widget is un-scrapeable — and when a Wix calendar widget won't render
> and Wix Events is empty, check the widget `appDefId` in `/_api/v1/access-tokens`; it may be a
> third-party calendar app.**

## 2026-07-01 — Harrier Central config-only BATCH (10 kennels) — 🇧🇷🏴󠁧󠁢󠁥󠁮󠁧󠁿🏴󠁧󠁢󠁳󠁣󠁴󠁿🇫🇷🇳🇱🇩🇪
- Scope: John asked to consolidate the remaining queued config-only HARRIER_CENTRAL kennels (ranks 12–21) into **one batch handoff / one PR**. Handoff `handoffs/2026-07-01-hc-batch-10.md`. Full per-kennel deep-dive; the 3 already-handed-off HC docs (Algarve/Moonshine/Hua Hin FM) left as-is.
- Outcome: **SHIPPED 2026-07-02** → [PR #2526](https://github.com/johnrclem/hashtracks-web/pull/2526) (merged) · retro `handoffs/retros/2026-07-02-hc-batch-10-retro.md`. Live in prod — **985 canonical events** across the 10 (bnh3 6 · newcastle 107 · aberdeen 268 · plympton 126 · toulouse 5 · tnth3 218 · dafth3 45 · filth 113 · sembach 47 · beerspoke 50). 🔴 **Shipped decision changed from the handoff:** ALL 10 kennels got a backfill + `upcomingOnly:true` (not just the 3 zero-upcoming) — a future-only HC kennel's past runs get CANCELLED as they age unless `upcomingOnly` clamps reconcile's window (retro §New-1); post-merge `cancelled=0` on all 10. - **Outcome: SHIPPED** → [PR #2196](https://github.com/johnrclem/hashtracks-web/pull/2196) (merged). NEW `KaohsiungHashAdapter` (static Cheerio, mirror `boiseh3.ts`) + 11 tests + new **Kaohsiung METRO** + self-hosted PNG logo. **4-round bot-review cadence** (own `/code-review` → Codex adversarial → Gemini nits → SonarCloud), each a pushed commit. Retro `handoffs/retros/2026-06-14-kaohsiung-h3-retro.md`.
- **Post-merge** (ran from the **main repo**, synced — stashed the user's doc-WIP on `docs/taipei-h3-ship`, `git checkout main` + `pull --ff-only`, seeded, restored WIP; seeding from the stale worktree would have reverted 5 other PRs' `sources.ts` config merged after the branch point): `prisma db seed` (additive — **Created 2 / Updated 412**) → `scrapeSource()` (no force) **eventsFound 2 / created 2 / 0 unmatched / 0 blocked / 0 cancelled / 0 errors**. Prod query confirmed kennel `kaohsiung-h3`, source + `SourceKennel` link with `config.upcomingOnly:true` intact, Kaohsiung METRO (`Asia/Taipei`, KHH), and 2 canonical events; `haresText` populated on both (the empty `hares` relation is the misman table, not scraping). Live page renders both runs + logo + founded year.
- Source: **HTML_SCRAPER (NEW `KaohsiungHashAdapter`, static Cheerio over fully-SSR'd Wix)** — `https://www.kaohsiunghash.com/run-information`. Parsed `/run-information` **only** (canonical, richest): the home page lists the same two runs and only adds the `#2733`-vs-`#2734` cross-page drift → skipping it removes the discrepancy entirely. Each run = a `#NNNN Month Day Title` `<h2>` + prose (time/cost/"Meet at …"/`maps.app.goo.gl`) + a `Your Hares:` `<h4>` whose names sit in the following `<h1>`. `config.upcomingOnly:true`, trustLevel 5, scrapeDays 90. Captured the verbatim DOM at build via `curl` (the research sandbox could only read rendered text).
- Dedup: full onboard — **442 slugs** (Chrome MCP 2026-06-14, the audit owed since 2026-06-13), no `kaohsiung*`/`khhh*`/`kh3` slug; only Taiwan-family slugs are `taiwan-h3`+`taipei-h3`. 🔴 kennelCode `kaohsiung-h3` (clear; bare **"KHHH"=Kampong H3**, **"KH3"=Kowloon H3** → both omitted from aliases — the city-based code/shortName discipline, same as `twh3-tw`/`taipei-h3`). est. **16 Sep 1973** (southern Taiwan's oldest).
- Metadata: founded **1973** (`/about`), mixed run types (Sat afternoon ~13:00 / Sat night ~19:00 / Sun family ~09:00 → flat fields + `scheduleNotes`; not over-engineered into `scheduleRules` from an image schedule), walkersWelcome true, FB page `KaohsiungH3` + IG `kaohsiunghash`. **hashCash left blank** (standard cash not published online — the NT$300 was a special joint-run `Event.cost`). Self-hosted Wix logo (`Hashlogopatch.png` → magic-byte-confirmed **PNG** 500×500, NOT trusting the `~mv2.png` suffix).
- Region: **NEW Kaohsiung METRO** under the seeded Taiwan COUNTRY — 2 `region.ts` edits (REGION_SEED_DATA + `STATE_GROUP_MAP`), sky palette like Taipei. 🟢 **COUNTRY_GROUP_MAP entry OMITTED** (the handoff said 3 edits; matched the Taipei precedent — Taipei is absent there and resolves fine, the metro key in that map is dead). `kaohsiung`/`高雄` inference already on `main`.
- Historical backfill: **none** — full 2026 schedule published only as an image (`KHHH V2(2).png`); no machine-readable archive (run #2732 implies deep history but the source exposes none). `upcomingOnly:true` protects the rolling ~2–3-run window from reconcile false-cancel.
- 🔴 Key learnings (→ retro): **(1) Codex adversarial review caught a real PARTIAL-drift hole** — the windowed-empty fail-loud guard only fires when the WHOLE result is empty, so one good run + one date-drifted heading would let `reconcile.ts` false-CANCEL the drifted run's sole-source canonical while the page still lists it. Fix generalizes the single-page guard to **per-numbered-block `ParseError`** (every `#NNNN` heading that won't fully parse → `errors[]` + `errorDetails.parse`). Reusable for any multi-run SSR page. **(2) Self-inflicted review churn** — a Gemini round-2 "use full month names" readability nit pushed `MONTH_RE` over Sonar **S5843** complexity (round 3). The form that satisfied ALL reviewers (and is genuinely best) from the first write would be a **generic word-token matcher (`/\b[a-z]{3,9}\b/gi`) validated by exact `Map.get()`** — no alternation (no S5843/S5852), no slice, no "Maybe"→"May" false positive, no object-injection. **(3) Seed from up-to-date `main`, never the stale worktree** (the `sources.ts` full-overwrite revert hazard, reinforced — 5 PRs had merged after the branch point).

- 🔴 Key learnings (→ retro): **the Lisbon/Porto HC coord work paid off a 3rd time** — the coarse `location:"SHANGHAI"` (`placeName===resolvable`) was dropped at the adapter (`dropCachedCoords`) → merge re-geocoded to the Shanghai centroid, **no adapter change**. **The handoff's `hares` field-fill prediction was wrong but harmless** — it claimed `stripTba` nulls "TBC", but `stripTba` strips only exact "TBA"; the `GEOCODE_FAIL_SENTINELS` set ("tbc"/"tbd"/…) is scoped to **location only, NOT hares** — yet the merge pipeline's own hare sanitization dropped "TBC", so the canonical event surfaced with **no hares** (clean; no adapter change warranted). **Bare city alias → kennel-specific** (Claude review): the seed's bare `"上海"` would mis-route every future Shanghai sibling → replaced with `"上海捷兔"` (Shanghai Hash, kennel-specific — mirrors Tokyo `東京ハッシュ`/Taiwan `台灣健龍捷兔`); bare `上海` stays in `COUNTRY_INFERENCE_RULES` only. **Bare CJK inference tokens are the file convention** (Gemini boundary-wrap declined) — the adjacent Taiwan rule uses bare CJK alternatives; `\b` is ASCII-only and can't bound CJK; for country inference a substring match is correct. → new HC city-sweep + Shanghai addenda in `source-platform-notes.md`.

## 2026-06-10 — Taiwan H3 (Taipei, Taiwan) 🇹🇼 first Taiwan kennel — ✅ SHIPPED
- **Outcome: SHIPPED** → [PR #2107](https://github.com/johnrclem/hashtracks-web/pull/2107) (merged). Live at https://www.hashtracks.xyz/kennels/taiwan-h3 (HTTP 200) — **3 upcoming canonical events** (weekly Sun 14:30: #2662 2026-06-14, #2663 06-21, #2664 06-28), all `CONFIRMED`. **Pure config-only HARRIER_CENTRAL** (zero new code, mirrors Lisbon/Porto/Hamburg H7) + new **Taiwan COUNTRY + Taipei METRO** region (all 5 `region.ts` edits, **sky** palette; 0 seed.ts — country→metro). No backfill. Three commits: seed/region base + CJK-inference fix (CodeRabbit) + S6035 char-class (SonarCloud). Retro `handoffs/retros/2026-06-10-twh3-tw-retro.md`.
- **Post-merge** (ran from the **worktree** on prod `.env`; main repo carried doc WIP): `npx prisma generate` (fresh checkout) → `prisma db seed` (additive — kennel/aliases/source + Taiwan/Taipei regions; **Created 1 / Updated 387**) → `scrapeSource(force:true)` → **eventsFound 3 / created 3 / 0 unmatched / 0 blocked / 0 errors**. Prod query confirmed kennel `twh3-tw`, source + `SourceKennel` link, both regions (Taiwan COUNTRY / Taipei METRO), 3 canonical events (#2662 real New-Taipei venue+coords `25.0147,121.6988`; #2663/#2664 default pin dropped via `hcGeocodeFailed` → Taipei centroid).
- Source: **HARRIER_CENTRAL (config-only)** — `harriercentralpublicapi.azurewebsites.net`, `publicKennelId: f1330d14-e3b4-427a-9bea-639f18218804` (`kennelUniqueShortName:"TwH3"`). All 3 filters (GUID / `cityNames:"Taipei"` / shortName) returned the identical 3-event single-kennel set. Verified via browser page-context fetch (Azure host allowlist-blocked from sandbox) — status 200; Claude Code reconfirms `adapter.fetch` from local env.
- Dedup: full onboard — no `taiwan*`/`taipei*`/`twh3*`/`formosa*` slug (live sitemap **436**, Chrome MCP 2026-06-10). 🔴 kennelCode **`twh3-tw`** — bare `twh3` is **Tidewater H3** (`kennels.ts:1431`); bare "TwH3"/"TWH3" alias omitted (lowercases to Tidewater's code → mis-route risk). Former name "China Hash HHH" also omitted as alias (globally ambiguous). slug `taiwan-h3` (ASCII, no pin).
- Events verified: **3 upcoming**, **2026-06-14 → 2026-06-28** (weekly Sun 14:30), verbatim #2662 "TwH³ 2662 - Low Hanging Fruit低垂的禁果" (hares "Only Grans;Shibari GayBlow", real New-Taipei venue/coords), #2663 & #2664 placeholder "No location provided" (Taipei default pin → adapter drops via `GEOCODE_FAIL_SENTINELS`). Run #2664 monotonic.
- Metadata: 台灣健龍捷兔, **one of Asia's oldest hashes, est. 1975** (taiwanhash.com/about; logo says 1976 — kennel's own correction; 50th-anniversary event 2025 corroborates). Weekly Sun, hares off **14:30** (pack ~3pm). hashCash gender-tiered **~NT$250 men / NT$200 women**, per-run variable (flagged). website `taiwanhash.com`, FB group `groups/taiwanhash`, Twitter `@HashTaiwan`. Logo ⚠️ self-host (taiwanhash.com WP `.png` or HC blob; confirm magic bytes).
- Region: **Taiwan NEW COUNTRY** — all 5 `region.ts` edits incl. `COUNTRY_INFERENCE_RULES` (ONH3-in-CI gap). country→metro (like Singapore/NZ) → **no seed.ts stateMetroLinks**. Sky palette (distinct from red/orange/green Asian neighbors).
- Historical backfill: **none** (HC future-only; ~2,600-run archive lives on the kennel's WordPress as unstructured prose recaps — sampled the homepage, no clean labeled fields / run# not in title → deferred, not dismissed unseen).
- Sibling sweep: HC `cityNames:"Taipei"` = TwH3 only. Distinct Taiwan kennels (Taipei Hash est.1973, New Taipei 新北捷兔, Taoyuan Metro 桃園捷運兔, Kaohsiung, 228 Hash) = separate future targets (worth an HC city sweep on Taipei/Kaohsiung/Taoyuan at refill).
- 🔴 Key learning: when onboarding a kennel whose bare shortcode (`TwH3`→`twh3`) is an existing **kennelCode** for an unrelated kennel (Tidewater), the suffix must go on the kennelCode (`twh3-tw`) AND the bare-shortcode alias must be dropped — kennel resolution does kennelCode-exact-match before alias, so a bare alias silently mis-routes. (Mirrors the Lisbon bare-`LH3` omission, but here the collision is a kennelCode not just an alias.)
- 🔴 Key learning (build-time, CodeRabbit): the handoff called CJK coverage of `COUNTRY_INFERENCE_RULES` "optional" because the English `eventCityAndCountry` ("Taipei, Taiwan") matches and #2662 carries explicit coords — but a Chinese-only location field (`新北市, 台灣`) still defaulted to **"USA"** under the ASCII-`\b` rule. Fixed in-PR by appending a CJK branch `[台臺][灣北中南]|新北|高雄` (the `[台臺]` class unifies the common/formal Tai- forms). For any CJK-locale country, **add the CJK branch, don't mark it optional** — and use a character class for single-char alternations or SonarCloud flags **S6035** (caught here; `(灣|北|中|南)` → `[灣北中南]`).

## 2026-06-09 — Lima H3 (Lima, Peru) 🇵🇪 first Peru kennel — ⏸️ NOT ONBOARDED (source dormant)
- **Outcome: NOT ONBOARDED (2026-06-10)** → `handoffs/2026-06-09-lima-h3.md`. The handoff's blocking live-verify gate **failed at build**: `fetchBloggerPosts` from local env reached the blog (blogId `4935356690439854808` — John was right it isn't deleted), but it is **dormant** — newest post **Hash 765, 2023-09-07** (~33 mo stale), only **8 posts / ~6 distinct runs** total (2018→2023, EN/ES pairs). Per the handoff's own "STOP and report back if dormant" rule + the no-dormant-source standard, **not onboarded**; user chose to abort and pick a new target (→ Taiwan H3 became the day's shipped kennel). Lima parked in Leads as dormant; no seed/code/region/PR.
- **Promoted to today's primary target at John's request** ("the point was to find live sources, not static schedules") — superseded the Budapest STATIC_SCHEDULE handoff below.
- Source: **HTML_SCRAPER (NEW `LimaH3Adapter`, Blogger API body-parse)** — `limahashash.blogspot.com`. 🔴 **Sandbox could NOT reach the blog by any method** (Blogger JSON feed + HTML + `?m=1` all empty; `site:limahashash.blogspot.com` = 0 indexed pages; Chrome MCP domain access denied; JSONP CSP-blocked). **John confirms it is live** → handoff flags Claude Code to `fetchBloggerPosts` from local env FIRST, confirm recency (every-other-Sat → newest within ~4wk) + real post structure before building. Field structure INFERRED from the sibling `limahash.com` Feb-2024 run post (English labels: When/Where/Trail/Theme/Hares/Hash Cash).
- Dedup: full onboard — no `lima*`/`peru*` slug (live sitemap 436, Chrome MCP). kennelCode `lima-h3` (bare `lh3`=London); bare "LH3" alias omitted.
- Events verified (build, 2026-06-10): **blog reachable via keyed Blogger API but DORMANT** — 8 posts total (2018→2023), newest Hash 765 on 2023-09-07 (~33 mo stale). Gate failed → not onboarded.
- Metadata: Lima Hash House Harriers — the **only hash in Peru**; every other Saturday ~16:00, hash cash **10 soles**, runs often start Miraflores; FB group `14448170326`. foundedYear unverified (flag). Logo self-host at build.
- Region: **Peru NEW COUNTRY** (5 `region.ts` edits, rose palette — 3rd South America country after Brazil/Paraguay; 0 seed.ts).
- Sibling: **Lima Extra Miércoles** (`limahash.com` WordPress, est. 2022, monthly Wed) = SEPARATE kennel — its WP REST works but is dormant (1 hash post Feb-2024) + linked Meetup dead → future source-add, queued in Leads, NOT bundled.
- Historical backfill: archive depth UNKNOWN from sandbox — assess via `fetchBloggerPosts(url,200)` at build; freeze a dataset if deep (Brasília pattern).
- 🔴 Key learning: "reachable" ≠ "active". A Blogspot can be unreachable from the research sandbox (empty feed/HTML + not Google-indexed) yet live for the user — making Claude Code's local live-verify the gating step was right. But the gate must check **recency**, not just reachability: here the keyed Blogger API returned data, yet the newest run was ~33 months old. The handoff's explicit "STOP if newest run is months old" rule did its job — honor it and abort rather than shipping a dormant directory entry. (Both Lima hashes — this blog + the *Extra Miércoles* sibling on `limahash.com` — are dormant; no live Peru source exists yet.)

- 🔴 Key learning (→ retro): the handoff's open coord decision ("accept the Lisbon-centroid pin" vs "drop coords") is really a **TWO-part change** — dropping the pin via `hcGeocodeFailed` is only half; `composeHcLocation` composes the location text independently (its `stripTba` doesn't filter "TBD"/"No location provided"/"ANNOUNCED LATER via Hares"), so coords-only leaves the placeholder string in `event.location` for the merge to store + geocode (Codex P2). Complete fix: a shared `GEOCODE_FAIL_SENTINELS` set drives BOTH `hcGeocodeFailed` (drop coords + `dropCachedCoords`) AND `composeHcLocation` (`stripPlaceholderLocation` → render unlocated). Seeded the set with the whole family (`tbd`/`tbc`/`to be determined`/`to be confirmed`/`to be announced`/`no location provided`/`announced later via hares`) per Gemini/Claude — config-free for future HC kennels. Suppression is EXACT-MATCH so a real coarse location ("Portugal" on #1025) survives. SonarCloud clean (set lookup, not regex). New Harrier Central section added to `source-platform-notes.md`.

Post-merge ran clean from MAIN/`main`: seed (additive — kennel/alias/source + Madrid region + scheduleRules), backfill **created=441 / blocked=0 / eventErrors=0**, forced `scrapeSource(force:true)` **created=1** (upcoming #2713) **/ updated=29 /** 0 unmatched / 0 errors. Retro `handoffs/retros/2026-06-06-madrid-h3-retro.md`.
- Source: HTML_SCRAPER (NEW `MadridHashAdapter`, self-hosted WordPress REST) — `madridhhh.com` (`/wp-json/wp/v2/posts`, cat "Run Directions"). Server-rendered, fully labeled run-post bodies (Run No./Date/Time/Fee/Location/GPS/Hares) + decimal coords. `config.upcomingOnly:true` (forward adapter pulls latest ~30; backfill owns the archive).
- Dedup: full onboard — kennel absent from seed + live sitemap (432 slugs via Chrome MCP; no `madrid*` slug; only Spain slug `mijas-h3`). kennelCode `madrid-h3` (clear; bare `MH3` omitted — collides Memphis/Munich/Montreal/Miami/Minneapolis).
- Events verified: live `adapter.fetch` — **30 forward events**, upcoming **#2713** "Habemus Papadam" Sun 2026-06-07 13:00, GPS 40.454352,-3.629372, hares "Bush Warmer, Sir Scrambled Dag", 0 bad rows.
- Metadata: founded **1984**, weekly Sun 13:00 + monthly 1st-Fri 20:00 (`scheduleRules`), 5€ (+2€ lift), FB page+group + IG. Self-hosted logo (`.png`, magic-byte verified).
- Historical backfill: **441 runs** (#2106 2015-06 → #2712 2026-05) — frozen `scripts/data/madrid-h3-history.json` + dumb loader. ⚠️ Handoff estimated ~550–600 to 2014; the REST feed actually exposes **453 posts → 441 past runs back to 2015-06-21** (no truncation — the month-archive widget over-counts). Coverage: hares 441/441, coords 418/441, location 435/441.
- 🔴 Key learning (→ retro): the handoff's "trust the in-body `Date:` line, NOT `post.date`" is right for the forward window, but the **11-year hand-typed archive** has 9/442 messy dates (year-less, a `Januray` month typo, copy-pasted stale day+year) that only surface on the full backfill. Fixed with `resolveRunDate`: parse anchored to the **publish date** (recovers year-less + typos), then re-anchor to the named weekday-on/after-publish **only when the parsed date is >45d BEFORE publication** (directional guard from Gemini/claude-review — an absolute `|gap|>45d` would corrupt a legit far-future announcement; measured: 0 far-future, 7 far-past in the archive). Review also fixed an `h`-suffixed-time parse (`7:30pm – 19:30h`→19:30; `9pm – 21:00 but be there for 20:45`→21:00), a `NaN` run-number path (require a leading digit), and reading the Maps URL from the anchor `href` with a **starts-with** selector (a `contains` selector matched a Facebook `l.php?u=…goo.gl/maps…` shim) + trailing-`)` strip. SonarCloud: 2 S5852 hotspots (theme + URL regex, both linear) marked **SAFE** via REST (the `sonarqube` MCP returns 0 PR hotspots); 3 S4325 `as never` → typed `madridSource()` factory. Prod domain is **hashtracks.xyz** (`.com` is parked-for-sale — stale `CLAUDE.md` example). Post-merge from MAIN repo (worktree has no `.env`).
- 🩹 **Post-ship cleanup 2026-06-09** → [PR #2050](https://github.com/johnrclem/hashtracks-web/pull/2050) (merged), CHROME_KENNEL audit #2040/#2041/#2039. **#2040 title/description SWAP (442/442):** the ship-time "synthesize title + theme-in-description" decision was a bug — `The "X" R*n` *is* the real per-event title. Adapter now routes the post title to `title`, `description: null`; backfill re-run **created=4 / updated=442**, prod now 0 placeholder titles / 0 stale descriptions (retro item #3 annotated REVERSED). **#2041 gap:** full WP-REST enumeration = 453 posts → 442 parseable runs (the "~10 missing" are posts with no `Run No.` label, not truly absent); curated **+4** real runs with explicit in-body dates (3× Jun-2025 + Xmas-2020), documented 6 dateless/COVID posts as excluded → **446 events**. Regenerated frozen JSON via new `scripts/generate-madrid-h3-history.ts` (full enumeration + shared `extractMadridPostBody`). **#2039:** foundedYear 1984 + self-hosted logo already correct (kept local over the audit's remote-URL suggestion). No orphan canonicals (date stable, sourceUrl-matched in place; the one same-day pair is a legit double-header #2335/#2336).

One source feeds both kennels via `Hash #` / `FMH #` line-prefix routing.

- Events verified: **11 upcoming**, 2026-06-08 (#2363, Miss Piss, GPS N48.21903 E16.37094) → 2026-12-13. Weekly cadence; stats page current "as of 2026-05-11".
- Metadata: founded **25 Apr 1982** (History page; homepage "43 Years" banner stale). €5 (adults; €2 youth; free kids/dogs). Mon 18:30 summer / Sun 14:30 winter + Full Moon Fri 19:00. Facebook live; blog dead since 2020. Self-host logo.
- Sibling: **Vienna Full Moon HHH** (`vienna-fmh3`, est. 5 Nov 2000) — included via `FMH #`-prefix routing on the same source (one source, `kennelCodes: ["vindobona-h3","vienna-fmh3"]`). FMH run numbering unreliable on source (#30? vs blog's #241 in 2020) — flagged. Blue Moon/New Moon excluded (ad-hoc, no cadence).
- Region: first 🇦🇹 Austria → 5-edit `region.ts` (Austria COUNTRY + Vienna METRO, teal palette) spelled out in handoff.
- Historical backfill: **none** — no clean per-run archive (blog dead 2020 #2085 / prose recaps; stats = per-hasher cumulative; locations = per-year Google My-Maps links; history = milestone prose). Deferred blog flagged low-priority only.
- Follow-ups (all done at ship): self-hosted both logos (magic bytes → `.png` + `.jpg`, NOT the handoff's guessed `.png`/`.png`; downscaled the 9229px PNG to 600px), `upcomingOnly:true`, per-kennel fail-loud zero guard, local `N<lat>,E<lng>` GPS parse. `source-platform-notes.md` "Vindobona H3 addenda" added (apex-not-www, ISO dates, N/E GPS, dual-kennel-by-prefix).
- 🔴 Key learning (→ retro): the handoff was unusually complete and every verbatim oracle held live (apex-not-www, `#30?`/`#23??` → undefined, the GPS pin). Review surfaced three latent edges, all fixed: (1) the zero-guard must measure the **raw parse**, not the date-windowed subset (Gemini/claude-review — a narrow `options.days` would false-fire drift); (2) the schedule run-cell match must use `extractHashRunNumber`, not `^#\d+$`, to absorb a prefixed `Hash #2363` (Codex — premise wrong, hardening right); (3) hand-rolled coord parses need an `isValidCoords` gate like every `geo.ts` path (my own `/code-review`). Plus the **österreich `\b` non-ASCII regex** dead-token (Gemini). Sonar S7755/S7735 fixed at source (no NOSONAR). **Worktree path gotcha AGAIN** (5th+ — region.ts edits landed in MAIN; recovered via `diff`→`checkout`→`git apply` into the worktree). Declined claude-review's "scheduleTime 24h" nit (12-hour is the convention for the legacy flat field).

Alias `"ASS H3"` OMITTED (collides with `ass-h3` Atomic Shit Show); all other aliases clear. 🔴 **`aliases.ts` is keyed by `kennelCode` (`asu-h3`), NOT slug** — the handoff said slug; the wrong key fails silently (no aliases attach). 🔴 **slug came out `asunci-n-h3`** — `toSlug("Asunción H3")` drops the accented ó to a dash; pinned `slug: "asuncion-h3"` in the seed (PR #1946).
- Events verified: **120 run posts** via WP.com REST (Chrome MCP; sandbox curl proxy blocks the API). Range **Run #1 (2021-11-24) → Run #120 (2026-05-31)**, all `categories:[1]`, no socials noise. **0 upcoming** at research time — onboarding on **recent-cadence evidence** (latest run **Sat 30 May 2026**, 4 days ago; biweekly Saturday ⇒ within 2× interval). Verbatim sample = Run #120 (full bilingual labeled body: hares "Ban the Cock", start Plaza Celsa Speratti, 15:30 meet/16:00 start, embed coords lat -25.30209/lng -57.60430).
- Metadata: foundedYear **2021** (5 Dec 2021, 3rd iteration — history page); hashCash **₲10,000 PYG cash** (default-run page); IG `asuncionh3` (FB none/flagged); walkersWelcome true; biweekly Sat ~16:00 (seasonal); stable wp-content logo (recommend self-host, confirm ext).

Generated via the adapter's exported `postToEvent` over the live feed; committed JSON confirmed byte-identical to the parser. Backfill applied post-merge: created=120, 0 errors.
- Implementation learnings (→ retro): (1) **Paraguay new-country 5-edit `region.ts`** (purple palette, `COUNTRY_INFERENCE_RULES` `paraguay|asuncion|asunción|luque`) — worked, no `inferCountry`→USA bug. (2) ✅ **embed-coord gotcha confirmed** — `extractCoordsFromMapsUrl` doesn't match the embed `pb` form; extracted in-adapter (`!2d`=lng/`!3d`=lat), `%21`→`!` URL-decode; exported `isValidCoords` from geo.ts to reuse. (3) 🔴 **date format FAR messier than the handoff's "parse the first D Month YYYY"** — only 10/120 use that form; the archive has ordinal+`of` (`5th of December 2021`), plain, English-weekday-+-Spanish-month (`14 marzo 2026`), full Spanish `de` form, and a recurring `Arpil` typo (#34/#35). Needed ordinal/`of`/`de` normalization + an EN+ES month map (incl. `arpil`). (4) **batch-posted runs** → parse in-body date not publish date (confirmed: #58–62, #84–86). (5) **safeFetch direct path has no default timeout** → added `AbortSignal.timeout(30_000)` (CodeRabbit). (6) FB URL still unconfirmed.
- **Refill check:** 6 rows still `queued` (VH3, Mexico City, Anchorage, Brasília, Bangkok Monday, KL Junior) → **>5, no refill needed.**
